<analysis>
The seed payload uses:
HTML sink: `<details>` tag with `ontoggle` event handler
JavaScript execution: Function call using `window['\x61lert']` (hex-escaped 'alert'), then building string via btoa/split/map/join chain
Detectable tokens:
Rule 934100: `String.fromCharCode`, `window` global, function construction patterns
Rule 941100: libinjection XSS detection (general structure)
Rule 941120: `ontoggle=` event handler pattern
Rule 941370: `window` global variable with bracket access pattern
Key mutations needed:
Change event handler (ontoggle) to avoid 941120
Avoid `window` with bracket notation to evade 941370
Avoid `String.fromCharCode` literal to evade 934100
Change overall structure to evade libinjection
Strategies:
Use different tags/events (img/svg onerror, form/input onfocus)
Access alert via different global paths (top, self, parent)
Fragment String.fromCharCode or use template literals
Use constructor chains instead of direct window access
Multi-stage decoding with atob/btoa
</analysis>

<seed_analysis>
The seed payload uses:
HTML sink: `<details>` tag with `ontoggle` event handler
Execution vector: IIFE that uses indirect eval via `(1,eval)('window')` to access global scope
Then accesses `alert` via hex escape `\x61lert`
Calls it with `String.fromCharCode(88,83,83)` to construct "XSS"
CRS 934100 detects: `String.fromCharCode`, `eval`, `function()`, `new Function`
CRS 941120 detects: `on[a-z]{3,50}` followed by `=` (the `ontoggle=` pattern)
CRS 941100: libinjection XSS detection
Key tokens to mutate:
Event handler name `ontoggle`
`eval` keyword
`String.fromCharCode`
Function construction patterns
The hex escape for 'alert'
After transformations (removeWhitespace, jsDecode, etc.), we must avoid the literal patterns.
</seed_analysis>
